# Ron Conway (p0015)

## Review approach

The SV Angel team biography identifies Ron Conway as founder/managing partner. Ron/Ronald and C/Crawford are supported by the specific SV Angel investment context. Numerous RON CONWAY/RETIRED/ENG records in Washington are deliberately not joined to the California investor. RONALD J and generational SR labels are conflicts needing review; even an SV Angel employer does not erase the conflicting initial. R. CONWAY/ANGELIST, SVA and ANGEL INVESTORS LC are not assumed to identify SV Angel. Generic self-employment requires a direct anchor. SELF EMPLOYED/ART DIRECTOR is rejected for a substantive professional mismatch.

Examined 159 candidate evidence groups containing 1096 source records. Accepted 273 record links. Counts are not distinct gifts or dollar totals.

## Identity and collision sources

- [SV Angel: Team](https://svangel.com/about/team) — Identifies Ron Conway as founder and managing partner of SV Angel. Observed 2026-09-20.

## Scope and unresolved work

Only the exact records and hashes listed below are covered. Known name variants and initials were retrieved across both installed cycles without a giving threshold. Unlisted surname typos, changed surnames and unitemized giving are not covered. Acceptance never changes reported employer or gift accounting. Initial decisions were prepared by Codex using explicitly documented person-specific criteria and then expanded to evidence groups; they are not human sign-offs.
